describe('PreferencesPanel', () => {
  function mockStatefulTheme(initialMode = 'light', initialTheme = 'flax') {
    useTheme.mockImplementation(() => {
      const [mode, setMode] = useState(initialMode);
      const [theme, setTheme] = useState(initialTheme);
      return { mode, setMode, theme, setTheme };
    });
  }

  it('renders Appearance section title', async () => {
    useTheme.mockReturnValue({ mode: 'light', setMode: vi.fn(), theme: 'flax', setTheme: vi.fn() });

function CompletedTaskRow({ task, listMap, lists, onToggleComplete, onDeleteTask, onEditTask }) {
  const list = listMap[String(task.task_list)];
  const editHandler = !isReadingList(list) && onEditTask ? (data, meta) => onEditTask(task.id, data, meta) : undefined;
  return (
    <div className={styles.taskRow}>
      <TaskCard task={task} listName={list?.name} listColor={getListColor(list, list?.idx)} lists={lists} onToggle={() => onToggleComplete(task.id, task.completed)} onDelete={() => onDeleteTask(task.id)} onEdit={editHandler} isCompletedSection />
    </div>
  );
}

const resetState = (preset = []) => {
  stateStore = [...preset];

const useStateMock = vi.fn((initial) => {
  const currentIndex = stateIndex;
  if (!(currentIndex in stateStore)) {
    stateStore[currentIndex] = typeof initial === 'function' ? initial() : initial;
  }

  const setter = (next) => {
    const prev = stateStore[currentIndex];
    stateStore[currentIndex] = typeof next === 'function' ? next(prev) : next;
  };

  stateIndex += 1;
  return [stateStore[currentIndex], setter];
});

({ event }) {
    const ref = useRef(null);
    const { openPopup } = useEventPopup();
    const tagContext = useTags();
    const accentColor = getEventTheme(event, tagContext).color;
    const badges = getTagBadges(event, accentColor);
    const readOnly = isReadOnlyEvent(event);

        <div ref={ref} className={`${styles.eventRow} ${readOnly ? styles.readOnly : ''}`}
            onClick={onClick} style={accentColor ? { '--accent': accentColor } : undefined}>
            <EventRowMain event={event} accentColor={accentColor} badges={badges} />

const isValidEmail = (email) => {
    const trimmed = email.trim();
    return trimmed.includes("@") && trimmed.split("@")[0].length > 0 && trimmed.split("@")[1].length > 0;
};
